HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_00a7bc1c33a786e716b4","text":"1. **Fire defined by an active intensification.** `ن و ر B002` supplies burning fire and its flickering movement, while `ح م ي B001` supplies heat and heating. Coming as the answer to 101:10, noun and adjective couple substance with operation: this is fire characterized by the production or extremity of heat. The heat branch does more than repeat the noun; it assigns escalating intensity. I infer an active process from the adjectival pairing, although the nominal clause states no event arrow. The ending reads as concentrated thermal action rather than a generic destination label.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000358/B006","root_000358/B012"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_118d84d172ac1ed28caa","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:11:2\",\"root_000358\",[\"B006\",\"B012\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Karanlık ve kararma boyutları, ateşi yalnız yükselen alev değil, uçurumu dolduran karanlık bir derinlik gibi gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000358/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3d7153137c587316aa20","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:11:2\",\"root_000358\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Zehirli yakıcılık boyutu, son ateşe yalnız çevresel sıcaklık değil bedene işleyen öldürücü bir zarar ekler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000358/B002","root_001564/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_6366047f9fd6b4211fcf","text":"3. **A repellent, exclusionary enclosure.** `ح م ي B002` supplies protection, prevention, and keeping others from a protected preserve; `ن و ر B006` supplies aversion, flight, and lack of steadiness. After the mother-source and abyss of 101:9, these branches turn the fire into a boundary that repels and confines rather than nurtures. The packet does not specify whether the prevention keeps the subject in or others out, so the direction of exclusion remains inferred. This reading changes `حَامِيَةٌ` from temperature alone into the operation of a hostile preserve.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000358/B003","root_001564/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8d494d5c971e6a157614","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:11:1\",\"root_001564\",[\"B007\"]],[\"101:11:2\",\"root_000358\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Öfke ve düşmanlık alevlenmesi boyutları, ahlaki çatışmanın dışarıda yaşanan bir ısı ortamına dönüştüğünü düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8d558933615a2167a353","text":"2. **The answer illuminates what the pronoun concealed.** `ن و ر B001` supplies light and illumination. The knowing question in 101:10 leaves `هِيَهْ` referentially opaque, and the fixed noun then identifies it. Illumination therefore becomes the fire's discourse role: the answer makes the abyss knowable even as its content is destructive. The packet supplies adjacency and the light branch; treating semantic disclosure as illumination is abductive. `نَارٌ` reads at once as the revealed object and the means by which the preceding obscurity is cleared.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000358/B002","root_001564/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9cfb2ff67fac38ed8265","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:11:1\",\"root_001564\",[\"B006\"]],[\"101:11:2\",\"root_000358\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Koruyucu alan anlamının tersine çevrilmesi, ateşi kişiyi tehlikeden uzak tutan değil kaçıştan alıkoyan kapalı bir bölgeye dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001564/B001","root_001564/B002","root_001564/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_af407d406a2dc520c9d1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:11:1\",\"root_001564\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ateşin aydınlatma ve işaret olma boyutları, önceki zamirin gizlediği uçurumu açıklayan şey ile cezalandıran şeyi aynılaştırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000358/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_b8660143388e1e382638","text":"No later ayah exists in the clipped focus window. The retrospective pass therefore introduced no later-triggered activation; the strongest surprise remains the earlier abyss in 101:9 activating the exploratory well-lining branch `ح م ي B011`.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ba6ae56fc23efd3b236f","text":"No later ayah follows. The retrospective movement here runs backward: the final fire reactivates 101:4's moths, 101:9's abyss, and 101:1-3's striking-warning as parts of one heat-light disclosure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000358/B001","root_000358/B002","root_000358/B003","root_000358/B006","root_000358/B007","root_000358/B011","root_000358/B012","root_001219/B010","root_001564/B001","root_001564/B002","root_001564/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d6d613d2d26621ef2cd3","text":"1. Fire as final illumination and burning. Evidence: ن و ر B002, burning fire; ن و ر B001, light/illumination; ح م ي B001, heat and heating. Structural cue: the last ayah finally answers the repeated ما. Mechanism: the hidden referent is disclosed by the same thing that burns; illumination and punishment coincide. Abductive move: I infer that final knowledge is experiential because the answer is heat-light, not definition. Reading change: نار حامية reads as the event's revelation, not only its penalty. 2. Protected zone that excludes escape. Evidence: ح م ي B002, protection/prevention and a protected preserve; ه و ي B002, abyss; ق ر ع B010, open road/courtyard; خ ف ف B002, rapid departure. Structural cue: the light one is sent to a \"mother\" and then identified as hot fire. Mechanism: Hawiyah is a bounded preserve in reverse: a zone one is kept in, not kept from. Abductive move: I supply the enclosure relation from حمى to the abyss-fire. Reading change: حامية means not merely hot but inescapably guarded. 3. Heated anger and hostile flare. Evidence: ح م ي B003, zeal/pride/anger; ن و ر B007, enmity flaring between people; ق ر ع B002, mutual combat. Structural cue: the surah begins with a striking event and ends with heated fire. Mechanism: conflict-heat is externalized as the final fiery state. Abductive move: I infer affective transmutation: social/moral contention becomes environmental heat. Reading change: the fire reads as the world of heated opposition made objective. 4. Black pit and well-lining. Evidence: ح م ي B006, black foul mud; ح م ي B011, well-lining stones; ح م ي B012, blackening of night/cloud; ه و ي B001/B002, void and abyss. Structural cue: \"Hawiyah\" as pit is immediately glossed by \"hot fire.\" Mechanism: the final place combines depth, blackness, enclosure, and heat. Abductive move: I infer a pit-well image from the adjacency of Hawiyah and حامية branches. Reading change: نار حامية is not only flame upward; it is a dark contained depth that burns. 5. Poisoning heat. Evidence: ح م ي B007, stinging poison and burning force; ف ر ش B005, insects drawn to fire; ب ث ث B001, scattered creatures. Structural cue: moth-like people appear before the final fire. Mechanism: what drew the swarm becomes a lethal burning force, like poison entering the body. Abductive move: I supply attraction-to-destruction from the moth/fire ecology. Reading change: حامية carries invasive harm, not only ambient temperature.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000358/B011"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ea23430800b6203176ce","text":"4. **Exploratory fiery lining of the abyss.** `ح م ي B011` gives `الحامية` as large stones used to line and secure a well, while `ه و ي B002` in 101:9 gives an abyss or pit. Their close sequence activates a spatial construction: the final fire can be imagined as the lining that defines or encloses the preceding depth. The packet supplies no well and does not locate the fire on the abyss's sides, so that placement is explicitly abductive. The terse answer then reads not only as what lies at an endpoint but as the burning boundary of the whole descent.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000358/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f63236d62071914347ee","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:11:2\",\"root_000358\",[\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Kuyu çeperini sağlamlaştıran taşlar çağrışımı, ateşi uçurumun yakıcı sınırı gibi tasarlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
